| 2026-05-11 | Docs/canon/rulebook.md (new), Docs/canon/rulebook-quick-reference.md (new), Docs/canon/README.md, .claude/skills/state-of-game-design/SKILL.md, .agents/skills/state-of-game-design/SKILL.md, CLAUDE.md, Docs/audits/2026-05-11-rulebook-architecture-assessment.md (new) | THR-403: Rulebook Canon Page Phase 1. New synthesis layer for the rules of play, written from the player's seat. Eight sections — What You Are, What the World Is, The Three-Beat Turn, What You Can Do, Your Resources, Encounters and Aftermath, The Clocks, Winning and Losing — each carrying inline `[IMPL]` / `[DESIGN]` / `[OPEN]` status flags with code-constant citations on `[IMPL]` claims and a per-section authority-boundary footer pointing back at UL (terms), the per-domain canon page (current spec), and Vision (why). Quick-reference companion at `rulebook-quick-reference.md` is the always-loaded board-game card (7 condensed sections, no flags, current rules only) per the 2026-05-11 refinement. Wiring: canon README index extended with rulebook + quick-reference rows and a synthesis-layer note; `state-of-game-design` SKILL gains a header reference pointing at rulebook as the primary synthesis surface for rules-of-play work; CLAUDE.md Canon Pages table grows two rows, Session Workflow design-work load order updated to load rulebook quick-reference always + full rulebook for rules-of-play work, Domain Skills table grows a "Rulebook (synthesis, always active)" row. First architecture-assessment pass at closeout written to `Docs/audits/2026-05-11-rulebook-architecture-assessment.md` answering plan §7's three questions (synthesis check — largely yes, one §5→§6 seam to watch; implementation-gap analysis — gap widest at Control sustain and Twilight→World-Soul pipeline; open-question blockers — none of the six current `[OPEN]`s blocks Phase 1). Six non-blocking `[OPEN]` questions surfaced and live in the rulebook for the next quarterly assessment per the THR-403 refinement's blocking-only filing rule. Phase 2 (THR-404 — 5 lint signals: ul, canon, code, vision, quick-reference-vs-rulebook) and Phase 3 (THR-405 — per-change trigger + monthly review + audit template) follow-ups were already filed by Cowork as Idea state blocked-by THR-403; they promote to Ready-for-Dev on close. Engine pillar N/A (documentation architecture, no runtime code). UI pillar deferred (in-game rulebook surface is a future project; the prose is written from the player's seat so it can render in-app later without a rewrite). Browser-verify exempt: docs-only change, no UI surface modified. Verification: `npx tsc --noEmit` clean, `npx vite build` clean (9.06s), `npm test` 724 files / 11072 tests pass (60.36s). | THR-403 closeout per Cowork handoff comment + plan doc `Docs/plans/2026-05-11-rulebook-canon-page.md` + 2026-05-11 review-pass refinement (estimate=5, quick-reference card added, OPEN-question discipline narrowed). The rules of play were previously distributed across `state-of-game-design`, Vision/, per-domain canon pages, UL shards, and plan docs — every agent reassembled them independently for every design decision, producing the silent drift that the Cosmology canon page's STALE-SOURCE WARNING already documents (7 stale surfaces at last count). The rulebook is the single synthesis surface that answers "what does the game do to the player on a turn?" without forcing the next agent to reassemble it from five sources. Status flags make the gap between `[IMPL]` and `[DESIGN]` structurally visible — the synthesis itself surfaces the prioritisation signal. The architecture-assessment cadence (the next pass scheduled for 2026-08-11, formalised in Phase 3 / THR-405) is the maintenance instrument that keeps the synthesis honest as the game evolves. |

Tags identify unambiguous positive/negative templates that the type-heuristic (Layer 2) and axiological fallback (Layer 3) cannot reliably classify. Rubric: Rule 2a (explicitly prosocial/lawful action → positive), Rule 2b (explicitly harmful/criminal action → negative), Rule 2c (sanctioned institutional duels → positive), Rule 3 (sole template in a type bucket). Tagged positive: 8 hire templates (recruit_militia, sway_mercenary, court_noble, rally_faithful, hire_guide, debt_collection, caravan_guard, guild_initiation_trial), 7 Lorekeepers scholarly templates, 4 Mercenary Company service/sparring/lifecycle templates, MC foreign_deal (trade), 1 Army compassion template. Tagged negative: 3 Underking Court criminal templates (fence_goods, blackmail_mark, black_market), MC trade_monopoly (predatory), secret.intercepted_message (threaten). Total: 27 tags, well under the 40-tag cap. ~126 L2-fallthrough templates left untagged (ambiguous or context-dependent per Rule 1). | Explicit tags act as Layer 1 override in the three-layer polarity cascade (determinePolarity in phaseReputationTraits.ts), ensuring high-confidence templates always route reputation correctly without relying on type buckets or axiological guess. |
